data-bibtex="%40inproceedings%7B%0Adoan2026instant%2C%0Atitle%3D%7B%7BINSTANT%7D%3A%20Compressing%20Gradients%20and%20Activations%20for%20Resource-Efficient%20Training%7D%2C%0Aauthor%3D%7BTuan-Kiet%20Doan%20and%20Trung-Hieu%20Tran%20and%20Enzo%20Tartaglione%20and%20Nikola%20Simidjievski%20and%20Van-Tam%20Nguyen%7D%2C%0Abooktitle%3D%7BThe%20Fourteenth%20International%20Conference%20on%20Learning%20Representations%7D%2C%0Ayear%3D%7B2026%7D%2C%0Aurl%3D%7Bhttps%3A%2F%2Fopenreview.net%2Fforum%3Fid%3DP2q6Y7UweV%7D%0A%7D"><font 4.0</font></font></a></span></div><div class="invitation-buttons"></div></div><div class="note-content"><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">关键词</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <span class="note-content-value"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">梯度压缩、激活压缩、资源约束训练</font></font></span></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">TL;DR </font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <span class="note-content-value"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">本文介绍了 INSTANT，一种利用低秩梯度和激活压缩进行高效训练的方法。</font></font></span></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">抽象的</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">深度学习以前所未有的速度发展，但也因此导致其复杂性显著增加。尽管加速推理的研究已相当广泛，但由于深度模型对计算和内存的高需求，如何在资源受限的预算内直接训练深度模型仍然是一个巨大的挑战。本文提出了一种名为 INSTANT（压缩梯度和激活以实现资源高效训练）的方法，旨在解决训练过程中的计算和内存瓶颈问题。INSTANT 通过将梯度和激活值投影到低秩子空间并在该压缩表示中执行计算，从而降低了反向传播过程中的资源需求。实验结果表明，INSTANT 能够有效地提高训练效率。</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="150" class="mjx-n"><mjx-c class="mjx-cD7"></mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn><font dir="auto" inherit;">降低计算成本和</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="151" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math 号提交内容的元审查</font></font></span></h4><button type="button" class="btn btn-xs permalink-btn" aria-label="复制备注 lS0jj7bGpw 的 URL"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-link " data-toggle="tooltip" data-original-title="Date created"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-calendar " aria-hidden="true"></span><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: class="revisions"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-duplicate " aria-hidden="true"></span><a href="/revisions?id=lS0jj7bGpw"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">修订</font></font></a></span></div><div class="note-content-container "><div class="note-content"><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">概括</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">本文最初收到两份负面评价（一份被拒稿，一份略低于接受标准）和一份正面评价（略高于接受标准）。在作者提出反驳意见后，审稿人 9WbD 表示将评分提高到 6 分，因为作者已经解决了其大部分疑虑。实际上，本文的主要问题在于：（1）与现有方法的比较不足（审稿人 FBNs 提出）以及讨论不够充分，尤其是在 CompAct 数据集上；（2）创新性不足且贡献过大（审稿人 FBNs 提出）；（3）缺乏在 GPU 上的实际训练时间（审稿人 jz4Y 和 9WbD 提出）；（4）对过采样超参数过于敏感（审稿人 jz4Y 提出）。</font></font></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">作者提供了详细的实验和解释来回应上述问题。针对FBNs提出的关于与CompAct比较以及创新性的质疑，作者从理论和方法层面给出了相应的解释，这些解释在元审稿人的理解范围内得到了充分的解答，尽管FBNs审稿人没有回复。此外，审稿人jz4Y和9WbD提出的关于GPU上实际训练时间和失败原因的质疑，在反驳中也得到了很好的解答。</font></font></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">总体而言，该论文的创新之处在于将激活值和梯度投影到低秩子空间，并在这些压缩表示中进行反向传播，从而加速模型训练。论文贡献扎实，并辅以全面的实验。在阅读了论文、审稿意见、反驳意见以及作者的回复后，Meta 审稿人建议接收该论文。</font></font></p>
</div></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">审稿人关注的问题</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">所有评论者提出的关于性能和 GPU 训练时间比较以及创新性的问题都得到了很好的解决。</font></font></p>
</div></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">评论者评分</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">如果审稿人积极参与讨论，可能会提高评分。</font></font></p>

<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">我们也根据审稿人的建议修改了稿件。</font></font><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">修改之处以蓝色标记</font></font></strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">。为了清晰地阐述我们的工作，我们撰写了这份总体回复，总结审稿人的意见和我们的回应。总而言之：</font></font></p>
<ol>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">我们已</font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">针对之前的工作（CompAct）提出了</font></font><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">所有疑虑。具体而言，我们重点阐述了我们在以下几个方面的差异：训练流程、压缩目标以及压缩方法。详情请参阅我们对审稿人意见的回复。</font></font></strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"></font></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">我们添加了额外的基线 Galore、CompAct </font></font><em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">（附录 H，第 1129 行）</font></font></em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">、ESPACE </font></font><em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">（附录 L.1，第 1377 行）</font></font></em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">、INSTANT-random </font></font><em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">（附录 K.2，第 1338 行）</font></font></em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">。与我们的 INSTANT 相比，它们的性能都较差。</font></font></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">我们补充了其他相关工作（Galore、CompAct），并展示了我们的差异</font></font><em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">（主论文第 101-107 行）。</font></font></em></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">我们补充证明，校准开销与训练开销相比很小</font></font><em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">（主论文第 810 - 833 行）。</font></font></em></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">我们提供了 V100 GPU 的时间报告</font></font><em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">（附录 J.2，第 1263 行）。</font></font></em></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">我们提供了关于复杂数据集（Places-365，包含 180 万张图像）的更多结果（</font></font><em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">附录 L.3，主论文第 1397 行</font></font></em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">）</font></font></li>
</ol>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">再次衷心感谢所有审阅者抽出宝贵时间。我们非常期待收到您对这些修改的反馈，并继续进行讨论。</font></font></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">作者</font></font></p>

<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">[2] GaLore：通过梯度低秩投影实现内存高效的 LLM 训练 – Zhao 等人，ICML 2024，arXiv:2403.03507。</font></font></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">[3] VeLORA：使用 Rank-1 子空间激活的内存高效训练 – Miles 等人，NeurIPS 2024 海报，将激活压缩到 1 维子空间的算法。</font></font></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">[4] Grass：使用结构化稀疏梯度计算高效的低内存 LLM 训练 – Muhamed 等人，arXiv:2406.17660。</font></font></p>

<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">[6] Bolei Zhou, Agata Lapedriza, Aditya Khosla, Aude Oliva 和 Antonio Torralba. Places: 一个用于场景识别的 1000 万图像数据库. IEEE 模式分析与机器智能汇刊, 40(6):1452–1464, 2017.</font></font></p>

的正式审阅</font></font></span></h4><button type="button" class="btn btn-xs permalink-btn" aria-label="复制备注 d1KApzSLwv 的 URL"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-link " data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" aria-hidden="true" data-original-title="Copy URL of note d1KApzSLwv"></span><span class="sr-only"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">复制备注</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">d1KApzSLwv的 URL</font></font></span></button></div><div class="subheading"><span class="invitation highlight" data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" style="background-color: rgb(255, 187, 187); color: rgb(44, 58, 74);" data-original-title="Reply type"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">官方评测</font></font></span><span class="signatures"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">评论者</font><span><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">FBNs</font></span></font> <span><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"></font></span></span><span class="created-date" data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" data-original-title="Date created"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-calendar " aria-hidden="true"></span><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">2025年10月31日 23:58（修改于2025年11月12日 11:37）</font></font></span><span class="readers" data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" data-original-title="Visible to &lt;br/&gt;everyone"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-eye-open " aria-hidden="true"></span><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">每个人</font></font></span><span class="revisions"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-duplicate " aria-hidden="true"></span><a href="/revisions?id=d1KApzSLwv"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">修订</font></font></a></span></div><div class="note-content-container "><div class="note-content"><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">概括</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">本文提出了一种名为INSTANT的训练时压缩方法，该方法将激活值和梯度投影到低秩子空间，并在这些压缩表示中执行反向传播。作者描述了以下三个步骤：（i）基于奇异值分解（SVD）的校准方法，用于构建逐层投影器；（ii）基于能量阈值和过采样的投影/截断方法；以及（iii）低秩反向传播算法。实验表明，在Transformer和CNN模型中，该方法显著降低了激活值内存和浮点运算次数（FLOPs），而精度损失很小。</font></font></p>
</div></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">健全性</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <span class="note-content-value"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">3：良好</font></font></span></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">演示</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <span class="note-content-value"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">4分：优秀</font></font></span></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">贡献度</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <span class="note-content-value"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">1：差</font></font></span></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">优势</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><ul>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">对不同模态（视觉和自然语言处理）和架构（Transformer、CNN）进行了可靠的实证评估。</font></font></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">清晰的阐述，注重可复现性的附录和伪代码。</font></font></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">实际意义：如他们的实验所示，通过合理的消融（过采样、校准频率、排序选择），可以减少激活记忆和反向 FLOPs。</font></font></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">为稳定的低秩训练提供了可靠的理论保证。他们的分析表明，基于奇异值分解的投影能够最小化重建误差，并且梯度近似误差在深度方向上保持有界，当保留能量 ε → 1 时，该误差趋于零。</font></font></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">包括与部署相关的实验（边缘设备/树莓派）。</font></font></li>
</ul>
</div></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">缺点</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><ol>
<li><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">与先前工作（CompAct，NAACL 2025，自 2024 年 10 月起可在 arXiv 上公开获取）存在严重重叠：</font></font></strong><br><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">概念上与 CompAct [1] 高度相似，结构上也与之平行，主要区别在于投影构建和校准选择，并且完全没有提及 CompAct，尽管后者在 ICLR 提交前几个月就已经发表并公开。两篇论文：</font></font></li>
</ol>
<ul>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">在前向传递过程中，通过低秩投影压缩激活。</font></font></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">计算压缩子空间中的梯度，然后解压缩以进行权重更新。</font></font></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">目标是同时降低内存和优化器开销。</font></font></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">在 LLaMA 和 BERT 类模型上验证其扩展性优势。INSTANT 的算法结构（前向压缩 → 压缩后的反向传播 → 解压缩更新）与 CompAct 的算法 1-3 相符。重叠之处还包括术语（“投影激活值”、“简化的优化器状态”）、理论依据和实证评估。</font></font></li>
</ul>
<ol start="2">
<li><p><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">创新性和贡献被过分夸大了：</font></font></strong><br><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">尽管 INSTANT 增加了一些实现上的改进，例如基于校准的秩选择和投影矩阵的选择，但这些只是工程上的扩展，而非概念上的进步。“首次联合压缩激活值和梯度”的说法并不属实，因为它与之前的工作存在重叠。</font></font></p>
</li>
<li><p><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">缺乏可比性：</font></font></strong><br><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">这些实验无法与最相关的研究（例如 GaLore [2]）或之后的大量研究（VeLORA [3]、Grass [4]、WeLore [5] 等）进行比较。所报告的基线数据不足以证明其创新性或优越性。</font></font></p>
</li>
</ol>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">参考</font></font></p>
<ul>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">[1] CompAct：用于内存高效 LLM 训练的压缩激活 - Shamshoum 等人，NAACL 2025，arXiv:2410.15352v1。</font></font></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">[2] GaLore：通过梯度低秩投影实现内存高效的 LLM 训练 – Zhao 等人，ICML 2024，arXiv:2403.03507。</font></font></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">[3] VeLORA：使用 Rank-1 子空间激活的内存高效训练 – Miles 等人，NeurIPS 2024 海报，将激活压缩到 1 维子空间的算法。</font></font></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">[4] Grass：使用结构化稀疏梯度计算高效的低内存 LLM 训练 – Muhamed 等人，arXiv:2406.17660。</font></font></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">[5] WeLore：用于内存高效微调的加权低秩投影 – Jaiswal 等人，ICLR 2025，arXiv:2407.11239。</font></font></li>
</ul>
</div></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">问题</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><ul>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">作者在提交论文时是否了解 CompAct (NAACL 2025)？</font></font></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">除了投影矩阵的选择之外，INSTANT 与 CompAct 之间还有哪些具体的概念或方法创新之处？</font></font></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">能否在匹配条件下（例如，相同的模型、排名、数据集）提供与 CompAct 的直接实证比较？</font></font></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">与随机预测基线相比，所声称的 FLOP 减少量如何？</font></font></li>
</ul>
</div></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">需进行伦理审查</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <span class="note-content-value"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">是，存在研究诚信问题（例如，抄袭、一稿多投）</font></font></span></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">伦理问题详情</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">该论文遗漏了对一篇高度相似的前期工作（CompAct）的引用，这引发了人们对其创新性主张的真实性和署名是否恰当的伦理担忧。只需在谷歌上搜索“LLM激活压缩”，CompAct就会出现在搜索结果的前列，因此不太可能是无意中遗漏了这篇论文。尽管实验和表述都很出色，但如果作者不提供与这篇高度重叠的工作进行全面且实验性的比较，我仍然会建议强烈拒稿。</font></font></p>
</div></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">评分</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <span class="note-content-value"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">2：拒绝，不够好</font></font></span></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">信心指数</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <span class="note-content-value"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">5：你对自己的评估结果绝对有把握。你非常熟悉相关工作，并仔细核对了计算过程和其他细节。</font></font></span></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">行为准则</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <span class="note-content-value"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">是</font></font></span></div></div></div><div class="ForumReplyForm_container__w2VwR"><div class="ForumReplyForm_buttons__eGXo_"><span class="ForumReplyForm_hint__azwm6"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="font-size: 0.75rem; background-color: rgb(62, 103, 117); border: 2px solid rgb(63, 105, 120);"><span><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">公众评论</font></font></span></button></div></div><div class="note-replies"><div class="note  depth-even" data-id="Owk9rFR79k"><div class="btn-group-vertical btn-group-xs collapse-controls-v" 不同（回应 1/n）</font></font></strong></h4><button type="button" class="btn btn-xs permalink-btn" aria-label="复制注释 Owk9rFR79k 的 URL"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-link " data-toggle="tooltip" class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">评论</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">首先，我们感谢您详尽的评论。关于您在</font></font><em><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">问题1、问题2</font></font></strong></em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">以及</font></font><em><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">伦理方面</font></font></strong></em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">提出的疑虑，我们想澄清如下：我们的工作</font></font><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">完全原创，不包含任何重复、抄袭或任何形式的学术不端行为</font></font></strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">。我们理解审稿人的担忧，但我们绝不会轻视此类指控，因为我们始终认为科学诚信至关重要。</font></font></p>
<p><em><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">→ W1 &amp; Q2 &amp; “伦理问题”：与先前的工作存在严重重叠（CompAct，NAACL 2025，自 2024 年 10 月起可在 arXiv 上公开获取）：</font></font></strong></em></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">我们的工作与文献[1]中引用的文献（CompAct，NAACL 2025）相关，因为两者都遵循相似的研究方向，即利用压缩方法解决训练过程中的计算和内存瓶颈。在这个领域，有多种方法利用梯度压缩、激活值压缩和/或优化器状态压缩。在此背景下，我们的工作与CompAct（以及其他相关工作）在几个方面有所不同。具体而言，INSTANT专注于在反向传播过程中压缩激活梯度的低秩梯度。从数学角度来看，CompAct和INSTANT在反向传播方面的区别在于：</font></font></p>
<ul>

<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">我们压缩了用于计算权重激活和梯度激活的方程，从而大大降低了反向传播过程中的计算成本。这是我们工作的创新之处，也是我们与 GaLore、CompAct 和 Grass 的区别所在，后三者都侧重于权重梯度的低秩。</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="156" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-mrow><mjx-mo class="mjx-sop"><mjx-c class="mjx-c28 TEX-S1"></mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-texatom texclass="ORD"><mjx-msub><mjx-mi class="mjx-b"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D420 TEX-B"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em;"><mjx-mi class="mjx-b" size="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D430 TEX-B"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-script></mjx-msub></mjx-texatom><mjx-mo class="mjx-n" space="4"><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D"></mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mfrac space="4"><mjx-frac><mjx-num><mjx-nstrut></mjx-nstrut><mjx-mrow size="s"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D451 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D43F TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-mrow></mjx-num><mjx-dbox><mjx-dtable><mjx-line></mjx-line><mjx-row><mjx-den><mjx-dstrut></mjx-dstrut><mjx-mrow size="s"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D451 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D464 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-mrow></mjx-den></mjx-row></mjx-dtable></mjx-dbox></mjx-frac></mjx-mfrac><mjx-mo class="mjx-sop"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29 TEX-S1"></mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-mrow></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mrow data-mjx-texclass="INNER"><mo data-mjx-texclass="OPEN"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">（</font></font></mo><mrow data-mjx-texclass="ORD"><msub><mi mathvariant="bold"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">克</font></font></mi><mi mathvariant="bold"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">西</font></font></mi></msub></mrow><mo><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">=</font></font></mo><mfrac><mrow><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">d</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">L</font></font></mi></mrow><mrow><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">d</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">西</font></font></mi></mrow></mfrac><mo data-mjx-texclass="CLOSE"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">）</font></font></mo></mrow></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">在优化器中。事实上，我们的工作</font></font><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">并未整合</font></font></strong> <strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">优化器状态。</font></font></strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">因此，我们的工作与所有对优化器状态进行压缩的工作（包括 CompAct）都不同。INSTANT 与 CompAct 的区别在于：</font></font></p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">方面</font></font></th>
<th><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">袖珍的</font></font></th>
<th><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">即时（我们的）</font></font></th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody><tr>
<td><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">压缩目标</font></font></td>
<td><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">激活 + 权重梯度 + 优化器状态</font></font></td>
<td><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">仅激活 + 激活梯度</font></font></strong></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">反向传播</font></font></td>
<td><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">只有权重梯度计算是在低秩空间中进行的。</font></font></td>
<td><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">权重梯度和激活梯度都在压缩空间中计算。</font></font></strong></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">储蓄来源</font></font></td>
<td><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">优化器状态和激活内存</font></font></td>
<td><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">反向 FLOP 减少 + 激活存储器</font></font></strong></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">投影</font></font></td>
<td><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">启发式</font></font></td>
<td><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">基于充分统计量的校准低秩子空间及其证明</font></font></strong></td>
</tr>
</tbody></table>
</div></div></div></div><div class="ForumReplyForm_container__w2VwR"><div class="ForumReplyForm_buttons__eGXo_"><span class="ForumReplyForm_hint__azwm6"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">添加：</font></font></span><button type="button" class="ant-btn css-1enej14 css-var-_R_5b_ ant-btn-primary ant-btn-color-primary ant-btn-variant-solid ant-btn-sm" style="font-size: 0.75rem; background-color: rgb(62, 103, 117); border: 2px solid rgb(63, 105, 120);"><span><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">公众评论</font></font></span></button></div></div></div><div class="note  depth-even" data-id="XUJ4WxzqNb"><div class="btn-group-vertical btn-group-xs collapse-controls-v" role="group" aria-label="折叠控件"><button type="button" class="btn btn-default "><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">−</font></font></button><button type="button" class="btn btn-default middle "><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">＝</font></font></button><button type="button" class="btn btn-default active"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">≡</font></font></button></div><div class="heading"><h4><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">（继续）INSTANT 与 CompAct（响应 2/n）不同</font></font></strong></h4><button type="button" class="btn btn-xs permalink-btn" aria-label="复制笔记 XUJ4WxzqNb 的 URL"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-link " data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" aria-hidden="true" data-original-title="Copy URL of note XUJ4WxzqNb"></span><span class="sr-only"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">复制笔记</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">XUJ4WxzqNb的 URL</font></font></span></button></div><div class="subheading"><span class="invitation highlight" data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" style="background-color: rgb(187, 187, 255); color: rgb(44, 58, 74);" data-original-title="Reply type"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">官方评论</font></font></span><span class="signatures"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">作者</font><span><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">&ZeroWidthSpace;</font></span></font> <span><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"></font></span></span><span class="created-date" data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" data-original-title="Date created"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-calendar " aria-hidden="true"></span><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">2025年11月21日 03:08（修改于2026年2月13日 01:38）</font></font></span><span class="readers" data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" data-original-title="Visible to &lt;br/&gt;everyone"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-eye-open " aria-hidden="true"></span><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">每个人</font></font></span><span class="revisions"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-duplicate " aria-hidden="true"></span><a href="/revisions?id=XUJ4WxzqNb"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">修订</font></font></a></span></div><div class="note-content-container "><div class="note-content"><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">评论</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">具体而言，这些差异还体现在以下方面：&nbsp;</font></font></p>
<ul>
<li><p><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">“计算压缩子空间中的梯度，然后解压缩以进行权重更新”：</font></font></strong></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">  反向传播过程中主要有两个矩阵乘法：权重梯度乘法和权重梯度乘法。</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="157" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-msub><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D454 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em;"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i" size="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D464 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-script></mjx-msub></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">克</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">西</font></font></mi></msub></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">用于更新权重和激活梯度</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="158" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-msub><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D454 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em;"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i" size="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D465 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-script></mjx-msub></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">克</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">x</font></font></mi></msub></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">用于传播回上一层。CompAct 仅计算</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="159" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-msub><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D454 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em;"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i" size="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D464 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-script></mjx-msub></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">克</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">西</font></font></mi></msub></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">在低阶空间中，同时保持</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="160" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-msub><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D454 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em;"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i" size="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D465 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-script></mjx-msub></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">克</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">x</font></font></mi></msub></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">在原始维度下（如图 2 和 [1] 的算法 2 所示）。相比之下，</font></font><em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">INSTANT 在低秩空间中进行两次乘法运算，从而极大地降低了反向传播过程中的计算成本，如我们的公式 (4) 和 (5) 所示。</font></font></em></p>
</li>
<li><p><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">“力求同时降低内存和优化器开销”：</font></font></strong></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">  本文仅关注在反向传播过程中降低内存使用量和计算成本，</font></font><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">而不像 CompAct（以及 Galore[2]）那样修改优化器</font></font></strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">状态。虽然我们的低秩投影机制原则上也可以应用于优化器，但这超出了本研究的范围。</font></font></p>
<p>  <em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">我们的工作利用了激活梯度的低秩特性。</font></font></em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">这是一种新颖的方法，其他工作中并未考虑，因此与其他相关工作（ComPact、Galore、WeLore、VeLore、Grass）有所不同。</font></font></p>
</li>
<li><p><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">“在 LLaMA 和 BERT 类模型上展示扩展性优势。INSTANT 的算法结构（前向压缩 → 压缩后的反向 → 解压缩以进行更新）与 CompAct 的算法 1-3 相匹配。……以及理论依据……”：</font></font></strong></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">  正如我们所指出的，我们的工作与 CompAct 的区别在于，我们并不以优化器状态的压缩为目标，而这正是 CompAct 的核心关注点。因此，两者的算法也存在差异。这一点也体现在我们的理论论证中。</font></font></p>
</li>
<li><p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">“</font></font><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">重叠之处还体现在术语上（例如“预测激活”、“简化优化器状态”）。”</font></font></strong></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">  我们并不使用这种特定的结构/术语。由于我们的方法侧重于低秩投影后的激活值，因此我们使用这些词语来解释它。我们不使用“简化优化器状态”，因为我们的方法不涉及这一步骤。我们仅在讨论相关工作 GaLore 的机制时才会提及它，GaLore 使用了该概念。</font></font></p>
</li>
<li><p><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">“……以及实证评价……”</font></font></strong></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">  我们沿用先前工作（Galore）中标准的实证评估设置，其中包括LLaMA和BERT类模型及数据集，CompAct似乎也采用了类似的设置。然而，更重要的是（且有所不同），我们扩展了评估设置，并在视觉任务上进行了实验，以进一步评估我们方法的有效性和通用性（类似于LBP-WHT）。</font></font></p>
</li>
<li><p><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">“通过前向传递期间的低秩投影来压缩激活：”</font></font></strong></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">  虽然 INSTANT 和 CompAct 都通过压缩激活值来减少内存占用，但 INSTANT 与 CompAct 的区别在于构建低秩投影的方式。事实上，这正是我们的主要贡献之一，因为我们提出了一种校准合适的低秩投影张量的新方法。</font></font></p>
</li>
</ul>
<p><em><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">→ Q1. 作者在提交时是否了解 CompAct (NAACL 2025)？</font></font></strong></em></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">我们在提交时就已经注意到了这一点。然而，我们将其视为 GaLore 在梯度压缩方向上的一个变体，这已在我们相关的工作中讨论过。我们的工作独立于 CompAct，并且两者是同时开发的。因此，我们未能将其纳入相关工作的讨论并提供更详细的比较，确实是我们的疏忽。这是</font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">关于此事的</font></font><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">唯一问题。</font></font></strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"></font></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">为了弥补这一疏漏，我们修订并扩展了这部分内容，添加了更多参考文献，并明确阐明了我们的方法与所引用论文之间的区别（第 101</font></font><em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">行</font></font></em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">至</font></font><em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">第 110 行，第 267 行</font></font></em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">至</font></font><em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">第 269 行</font></font></em><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">）。我们还在附录 H 中扩展了我们的基线，如回复 4/n 所示。</font></font></p>
</div></div></div></div><div class="ForumReplyForm_container__w2VwR"><div class="ForumReplyForm_buttons__eGXo_"><span class="ForumReplyForm_hint__azwm6"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">添加：</font></font></span><button type="button" class="ant-btn css-1enej14 css-var-_R_5b_ ant-btn-primary ant-btn-color-primary ant-btn-variant-solid ant-btn-sm" style="font-size: 0.75rem; background-color: rgb(62, 103, 117); border: 2px solid rgb(63, 105, 120);"><span><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font inherit;">＝</font></font></button><button type="button" class="btn btn-default active"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">≡</font></font></button></div><div class="parent-title"><h5><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-share-alt " aria-hidden="true"></span><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">回复</font></font> <font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">（继续）INSTANT 与 CompAct（响应 2/n）不同</font></font></h5></div><div class="heading"><h4><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">解决新颖性问题并证明我们基于数学的预测的重要性（回复 3/n）</font></font></strong></h4><button type="button" class="btn btn-xs permalink-btn" aria-label="复制备注 C3L0bxIWch 的 URL"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-link " data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" aria-hidden="true" data-original-title="Copy URL of note C3L0bxIWch"></span><span class="sr-only"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">复制备注</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">C3L0bxIWch的 URL</font></font></span></button></div><div class="subheading"><span class="invitation highlight" data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" style="background-color: rgb(187, 187, 255); color: rgb(44, 58, 74);" data-original-title="Reply type"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">官方评论</font></font></span><span class="signatures"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">作者</font><span><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">&ZeroWidthSpace;</font></span></font> <span><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"></font></span></span><span class="created-date" data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" data-original-title="Date created"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-calendar " aria-hidden="true"></span><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">2025年11月21日 03:09（修改于2026年2月13日 01:38）</font></font></span><span class="readers" data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" data-original-title="Visible to &lt;br/&gt;everyone"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-eye-open " aria-hidden="true"></span><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">每个人</font></font></span><span class="revisions"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-duplicate " aria-hidden="true"></span><a href="/revisions?id=C3L0bxIWch"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">修订</font></font></a></span></div><div class="note-content-container "><div class="note-content"><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">评论</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><p><em><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">→ W2.1：“声称自己是‘第一个同时压缩激活值和梯度’的人是错误的”</font></font></strong></em> </p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">我们想强调的是，</font></font><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">我们是第一个利用许多数据分布中激活梯度的低秩特性的人</font></font></strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">，而这在以前关于压缩权重（WeLore [5]）、激活（CompAct [1]、VeLORA [3]）和优化器状态和权重梯度（CompAct [1]、GaLore[2]、Grass[4]）的工作中都没有被探索过。</font></font></p>
<p><em><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">→ W2.2：“虽然 INSTANT 增加了基于校准的秩选择等实现改进，并改变了投影矩阵的选择，但这些是工程扩展，而不是概念上的进步”。</font></font></strong></em> </p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">我们的两个基线方法，梯度滤波和 LBP-WHT，虽然压缩了激活值和激活梯度，但它们的方法基于输入数据的低频假设，并且只在图像任务上效果良好。</font></font></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">我们基于校准的秩选择策略的创新之处在于，它能够</font></font><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">为激活值和激活梯度识别合适的低秩子空间，该策略建立在充分统计（第3.2节）和清晰证明（附录B、C）的数学基础之上。</font></font></strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">这使得INSTANT能够在保留关键信息的同时，将张量投影到更紧凑的空间中，从而确保效率和最优的低秩表示。</font></font></p>
<p><em><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">→ 问题 4：所声称的 FLOP 减少量与随机投影基线相比如何？</font></font></strong></em></p>
<p><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">理论上，随机投影不能保证良好的逼近效果</font></font></strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">，这促使我们构建自适应子空间（附录 B.1）。</font></font></p>
<p><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">经验表明，随机投影会导致性能下降。</font></font></strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">我们进行了额外的实验来评估基于校准的排序选择的有效性。具体而言，我们将我们的方法与随机投影基线方法进行了比较，并观察到校准始终能产生更好的低秩表示。</font></font></p>

<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">实验结果表明，当压缩秩设置为时，INSTANT 方法的性能显著优于随机投影方法。</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="161" style="font-size: 121.3%; 3/n）</font></font></h5></div><div class="heading"><h4><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">通过与 CompAct、GaLore（响应速度 4/n）的额外比较，展现即时效率。</font></font></strong></h4><button type="button" class="btn btn-xs permalink-btn" aria-label="复制笔记 9C6D7Ny8vf 的 URL"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-link " data-toggle="tooltip" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">修订</font></font></a></span></div><div class="note-content-container "><div class="note-content"><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">评论</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><p><em><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">→ W3：“缺乏比较”：这些实验无法与最合适的相关作品（如 GaLore [2]）或之后的无数作品（VeLORA [3]、Grass [4]、WeLore [5]……）进行比较。</font></font></strong></em> </p>
<p>In our submission, to evaluate the effectiveness of our method, we chose Gradient-Filter and LBP-WHT as baselines, since these papers also discovered the low-rank compression of <strong>both activation and activation gradient,</strong> which differs from CompAct[1], GaLore[2], VeLORA[3], Grass[4], WeLore[5] as stated in Response 3/n.</p>
<p>Understanding the reviewer, as well as other readers’ concerns about the comparison between our method and the optimizer gradient compression method, we provide the ablation study of our method compared to Galore[2] (compress weight gradient, optimizer state) and CompAct[1] (compress activation, weight gradient, optimizer state) in Appendix H (<em>line 1128</em> to <em>line 1167</em>) and analyze the strengths, weaknesses of these methods, and suggest the combination of INSTANT with these methods. <em><strong>We want to note that CompAct code wasn't available, so we reimplemented it based on the paper. We provided implementation in the updated supplementary.</strong></em></p>

<p>From the two tables above, we observe that GaLore consistently achieves better performance than CompAct under the same low-rank constraint. However, because GaLore primarily targets the optimizer states, its activation memory consumption and computational cost remain similar to Vanilla training. In contrast, CompAct substantially reduces activation memory by compressing activations during the forward pass. Compared to CompAct, under the same activation memory budget, INSTANT is able to save a large portion of the backward computational cost while achieving better performance.</p>
<p>However, because CompAct also compresses the optimizer states, it explores a complementary dimension to INSTANT. In principle, the two approaches can be combined to simultaneously reduce activation memory, optimizer-state memory, and computational cost. We further believe that our calibration-based projection can, at least theoretically, be extended to compress optimizer states as well.</p>
</div></div></div></div><div class="ForumReplyForm_container__w2VwR"><div class="ForumReplyForm_buttons__eGXo_"><span class="ForumReplyForm_hint__azwm6">Add:</span><button type="button" class="ant-btn css-1enej14 css-var-_R_5b_ ant-btn-primary ant-btn-color-primary ant-btn-variant-solid ant-btn-sm" style="font-size: 0.75rem; background-color: rgb(62, 103, 117); border: 2px solid rgb(63, 105, 120);"><span>Public Comment</span></button></div></div></div></div></div><div class="note  depth-odd" data-id="3SMPnWuqvm"><div class="btn-group-vertical btn-group-xs collapse-controls-v" role="group" aria-label="折叠控件"><button type="button" class="btn btn-default ">−</button><button type="button" class="btn btn-default middle ">＝</button><button type="button" class="btn btn-default active">≡</button></div><div class="heading"><h4><span>Official Review of Submission6348 by Reviewer jz4Y</span></h4><button type="button" class="btn btn-xs permalink-btn" aria-label="复制备注 3SMPnWuqvm 的 URL"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-link " data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" aria-hidden="true" data-original-title="Copy URL of note 3SMPnWuqvm"></span><span class="sr-only">Copy URL of note 3SMPnWuqvm</span></button></div><div class="subheading"><span class="invitation highlight" data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" style="background-color: rgb(255, 187, 187); color: rgb(44, 58, 74);" data-original-title="Reply type">Official Review</span><span class="signatures">by <span>Reviewer jz4Y</span></span><span class="created-date" data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" data-original-title="Date created"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-calendar " aria-hidden="true"></span>31 Oct 2025, 05:27 (modified: 12 Nov 2025, 11:37)</span><span class="readers" data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" data-original-title="Visible to &lt;br/&gt;everyone"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-eye-open " aria-hidden="true"></span>Everyone</span><span class="revisions"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-duplicate " aria-hidden="true"></span><a href="/revisions?id=3SMPnWuqvm">Revisions</a></span></div><div class="note-content-container "><div class="note-content"><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Summary:</strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><p>This paper proposes a compression method called INSTANT. INSTANT is a method that projects the activations and gradients to lower ranks, based of analysis that is done at regular intervals during training. The authors show the benefits of compressing both the gradients and activations during training by showing reduction in FLOPs and training time (in some cases).</p>
</div></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Soundness:</strong> <span class="note-content-value">2: fair</span></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Presentation:</strong> <span class="note-content-value">3: good</span></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Contribution:</strong> <span class="note-content-value">3: good</span></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Strengths:</strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><ol>
<li><p>The paper is  tackling both memory and computational bottlenecks.  Activation compression for memory saving has been explored previously, but the idea of also compressing the activation gradient (<mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="164" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-msub><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D454 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em;"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i" size="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D466 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-script></mjx-msub></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>g</mi><mi>y</mi></msub></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container>) to reduce backpropagation FLOPs is a useful contribution.</p>
</li>
<li><p>The authors provide empirical evidence (Fig. 3) to support their intuition that activation gradients are inherently low-rank, justifying their compression approach.</p>
</li>
<li><p>NSTANT's  SVD-based approach is data-driven. This allows it to generalize more effectively to other modalities, as demonstrated by its strong performance on NLP tasks (Table 2) where previous works (eg LBP-WHT) struggles.</p>
</li>
<li><p>The authors validates the method's usability by reporting significant wall-clock speedups (2x to 12.5x) on resource-constrained edge CPUs.</p>
</li>
</ol>
</div></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Weaknesses:</strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><p>I believe, currently this paper's primary weaknesses lie in the evaluation of its computational claims and the transparency of its overhead costs.</p>
<p><strong>Omission of Wall-Clock Training Time on GPUs:</strong></p>
<ol>
<li>The paper's main results (Tables 1 &amp; 2) were generated on an NVIDIA V100 GPU but only report FLOPs, not wall-clock training time.</li>
</ol>
<p>a) The authors' justification (Section 4.1) that "FLOPs... [are] unaffected by implementation details" is a weak defense. A reduction in FLOPs does not guarantee a proportional reduction in training time, especially on GPUs. Modern deep learning libraries (like cuDNN) are highly optimized for large, dense matrix multiplications, and the low-rank operations introduced by INSTANT may not be as implementation-efficient, thus creating a bottleneck.</p>
<p>b) The authors themselves admit a discrepancy in Appendix I, stating, "The (12x) time reduction is not comparable to (17x) FLOP reduction," even on a CPU. This gap is likely to be even larger on a GPU, and the lack of this data makes it difficult to assess the practical speedup in a typical training environment.</p>
<p><strong>Cost and Nature of the "Static" Subspace:</strong></p>
<ol start="2">
<li>The term "static" subspace (used in the contribution list and abstract) could be misleading. The subspace is not fixed; it is "periodically" recalibrated every <mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="165" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-msub><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D441 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em; margin-left: -0.085em;"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i" size="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D461 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-script></mjx-msub></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>N</mi><mi>t</mi></msub></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container> steps (e.g., <mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="166" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-msub><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D441 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em; margin-left: -0.085em;"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i" size="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D461 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-script></mjx-msub><mjx-mo class="mjx-n" space="4"><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D"></mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn class="mjx-n" space="4"><mjx-c class="mjx-c35"></mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30"></mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>N</mi><mi>t</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>50</mn></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container> or <mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="167" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-msub><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D441 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em; margin-left: -0.085em;"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i" size="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D461 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-script></mjx-msub><mjx-mo class="mjx-n" space="4"><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D"></mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn class="mjx-n" space="4"><mjx-c class="mjx-c32"></mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30"></mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30"></mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>N</mi><mi>t</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>200</mn></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container>).</li>
</ol>
<p>a) The computational cost of this recalibration (Algorithm 1) appears to be non-trivial and is not accounted for in the reported per-step FLOP savings. This calibration requires running multiple batches and performing SVD for every layer being compressed. This overhead could significantly diminish the overall wall-clock time savings.</p>
<p>b) The method's stability relies on an "oversampling" hyperparameter <mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="168" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D45D TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container>, which is introduced to "reduce information loss when the core bases change" between calibrations (Section 3.2, Fig. 6). This suggests the "static" assumption is fragile and introduces another sensitive hyperparameter that must be tuned, adding to the method's complexity.</p>
</div></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Questions:</strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><ol>
<li><p>Could the authors please provide the total wall-clock training time (not just backward time) for the main V100 GPU experiments in Tables 1 and 2? This is essential for evaluating the practical speedup of INSTANT in a standard training scenario.</p>
</li>
<li><p>How is the computational cost of the periodic calibration step (Algorithm 1) factored into the total FLOPs reported? Could you provide an analysis of this overhead, for instance, as a percentage of total training time or FLOPs?</p>
</li>
<li><p>Given that the subspace is recalibrated every <mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="169" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-msub><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D441 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em; margin-left: -0.085em;"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i" size="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D461 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-script></mjx-msub></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>N</mi><mi>t</mi></msub></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container> steps, would "periodically updated" or "cached" be a more accurate description than "static"?</p>
</li>
<li><p>The oversampling parameter <mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="170" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D45D TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container> appears critical for performance (Fig. 6, Fig. 8). How sensitive is the method to the choice of <mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="171" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D45D TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container> and the calibration frequency <mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="172" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-msub><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D441 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em; margin-left: -0.085em;"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i" size="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D461 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-script></mjx-msub></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>N</mi><mi>t</mi></msub></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container>? Does this introduce a significant hyperparameter tuning burden?</p>
</li>
<li><p>Following up on the 17x FLOP vs. 12x time gap noted in Appendix I: What is the authors' hypothesis for the performance gap on a V100, where highly optimized dense matrix multiplication kernels are the standard?</p>
</li>
</ol>
</div></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Flag For Ethics Review:</strong> <span class="note-content-value">No ethics review needed.</span></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Rating:</strong> <span class="note-content-value">6: marginally above the acceptance threshold. But would not mind if paper is rejected</span></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Confidence:</strong> <span class="note-content-value">4: You are confident in your assessment, but not absolutely certain. It is unlikely, but not impossible, that you did not understand some parts of the submission or that you are unfamiliar with some pieces of related work.</span></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Code Of Conduct:</strong> <span class="note-content-value">Yes</span></div></div></div><div class="ForumReplyForm_container__w2VwR"><div class="ForumReplyForm_buttons__eGXo_"><span class="ForumReplyForm_hint__azwm6">Add:</span><button type="button" class="ant-btn css-1enej14 css-var-_R_5b_ ant-btn-primary ant-btn-color-primary ant-btn-variant-solid ant-btn-sm" style="font-size: 0.75rem; background-color: rgb(62, 103, 117); border: 2px solid rgb(63, 105, 120);"><span>Public Comment</span></button></div></div><div class="note-replies"><div class="note  depth-even" data-id="1etx1THi9M"><div class="btn-group-vertical btn-group-xs collapse-controls-v" role="group" aria-label="折叠控件"><button type="button" class="btn btn-default ">−</button><button type="button" class="btn btn-default middle ">＝</button><button type="button" class="btn btn-default active">≡</button></div><div class="heading"><h4><strong>Analysis of Training time on GPU and hypothesis about the time increase (Response 1/n)</strong></h4><button type="button" class="btn btn-xs permalink-btn" aria-label="复制备注 1etx1THi9M 的 URL"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-link " data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" aria-hidden="true" data-original-title="Copy URL of note 1etx1THi9M"></span><span class="sr-only">Copy URL of note 1etx1THi9M</span></button></div><div class="subheading"><span class="invitation highlight" data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" style="background-color: rgb(187, 187, 255); color: rgb(44, 58, 74);" data-original-title="Reply type">Official Comment</span><span class="signatures">by <span>Authors</span></span><span class="created-date" data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" data-original-title="Date created"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-calendar " aria-hidden="true"></span>21 Nov 2025, 03:21 (modified: 13 Feb 2026, 01:38)</span><span class="readers" data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" data-original-title="Visible to &lt;br/&gt;everyone"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-eye-open " aria-hidden="true"></span>Everyone</span><span class="revisions"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-duplicate " aria-hidden="true"></span><a href="/revisions?id=1etx1THi9M">Revisions</a></span></div><div class="note-content-container "><div class="note-content"><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Comment:</strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><p>We appreciate the Reviewer’s comprehensive comments. As you suggested (<em>W1 &amp; Q1 &amp; Q5</em>), we did experiments to get the training time of INSTANT on V100 GPU.</p>
<p><em><strong>→ W1: “Omission of Wall-Clock Training Time on GPUs”</strong></em></p>
<p><em><strong>→ Q1: “Could the authors please provide the total wall-clock training time (not just backward time) for the main V100 GPU experiments in Tables 1 and 2?”</strong></em></p>
<p>We conducted experiments on the framework of MMCV, model EfficientFormerL1, with datasets CIFAR10, and with all methods as provided in Tab.1 <em>(line 340, main paper)</em> (LBP-WHT, Gradient Filtering, INSTANT (ours), Vanilla). We observed similar results of CIFAR100. Due to the limited time of Rebuttal, we just ran experiments with these 2 datasets.</p>

<p>&nbsp;<em>( * ) <strong>Calibration time</strong> is <strong>total additional time</strong> used for creating periodically updated subspaces during 1 training epoch ( ** ) <strong>Backward time</strong> is total time of <code>loss.backward()</code> during 1 training epoch. ( *** ) <strong>Epoch time</strong> is the <strong>total running time</strong> of training 1 epoch, including calibration time (with INSTANT), forward pass, activation savings, loss calculation, backward pass, optimizer update,….. These reported numbers are averaged over 10 training epochs.</em></p>
<p>On GPU V100, <strong>INSTANT’s backward time is 1.4x compared to Vanilla backwards time</strong>. The total training time (epoch time) of INSTANT is slightly higher than Vanilla. In short, INSTANT’s FLOP reductions are not converted into time reduction like training on CPU.</p>
<p><em><strong>→ Q5: “Following up on the 17x FLOP vs. 12x time gap noted in Appendix I: What is the authors' hypothesis for the performance gap on a V100”</strong></em></p>
<p>As shown in the table, contrary to time reduction on CPU <em>(line 427, main paper)</em>, INSTANT slightly increases training time on GPU V100. We believe this gap is due to that CUDA is optimized for large matrices (as the Reviewer suggested), and kernel launching overhead.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>CUDA optimized for large matrices</strong> is the main reason for the slower INSTANT implementation on the GPU. A GPU with multiple cores is designed for parallel computations of large operations. Splitting a large operation into three smaller ones lowers the utilization of GPU cores and leads to overhead.</li>
<li><strong>Kernel launching overhead and Memory bandwidth</strong>: INSTANT low-rank <mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="173" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D466 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo class="mjx-n" space="4"><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D"></mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo class="mjx-n" space="4"><mjx-c class="mjx-c28"></mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D465 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo class="mjx-n" space="3"><mjx-c class="mjx-c22C5"></mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-msup space="3"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D443 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: 0.363em; margin-left: 0.055em;"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i" size="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D447 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-script></mjx-msup><mjx-mo class="mjx-n"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29"></mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo class="mjx-n" space="3"><mjx-c class="mjx-c22C5"></mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo class="mjx-n" space="3"><mjx-c class="mjx-c28"></mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D443 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo class="mjx-n" space="3"><mjx-c class="mjx-c22C5"></mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-msup space="3"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D464 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: 0.363em;"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i" size="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D447 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-script></mjx-msup><mjx-mo class="mjx-n"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29"></mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>⋅</mo><msup><mi>P</mi><mi>T</mi></msup><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>⋅</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>⋅</mo><msup><mi>w</mi><mi>T</mi></msup><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container> launches 3 CUDA kernels for 3 multiplications, while Vanilla <mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="174" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D466 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo class="mjx-n" space="4"><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D"></mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi class="mjx-i" space="4"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D465 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo class="mjx-n" space="3"><mjx-c class="mjx-c22C5"></mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-msup space="3"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D464 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: 0.363em;"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i" size="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D447 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-script></mjx-msup></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>⋅</mo><msup><mi>w</mi><mi>T</mi></msup></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container> requires launching only 1 CUDA kernel.  Moreover, specialized memory types like GDDR6 or HBM (High Bandwidth Memory) lead to rapid data transfers and access within the GPU, which reduces the time for large matrix loading.</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Why INSTANT gains on CPU?</strong> Different from parallel computing on GPU with multiple cores, CPU has much smaller number of cores so it cannot compute a large computation at once. Moreover, in Von Neumann architecture, CPU needs to access data from cache/memory. Small matrices can be loaded from cache, which reduces time, while loading a large matrix from memory takes additional time.   </p>
<p>In short, both devices have the gain of smaller arithmetic multiplication, but this gain is small compared to the extra cost of kernel launching overhead and memory bandwidth. </p>
<p>We would like to thank you for the insightful question, which helps highlight the applicability of our work from additional perspective. We updated our paper <em>(line 431, line 1263)</em> to include this discussion. Nevertheless, we we will definitely focus on this topic in future work.</p>

color: rgb(44, 58, 74);" data-original-title="Reply type">Official Comment</span><span class="signatures">by <span>Authors</span></span><span class="created-date" data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" data-original-title="Date created"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-calendar " aria-hidden="true"></span>21 Nov 2025, 03:23 (modified: 13 Feb 2026, 01:38)</span><span class="readers" data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" data-original-title="Visible to &lt;br/&gt;everyone"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-eye-open " aria-hidden="true"></span>Everyone</span><span class="revisions"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-duplicate " aria-hidden="true"></span><a href="/revisions?id=AyNxuCUyja">Revisions</a></span></div><div class="note-content-container "><div class="note-content"><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Comment:</strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><p><em><strong>→  "W2 &amp; Q2: "How is the computational cost of the periodic calibration step (Algorithm 1) factored into the total FLOPs reported? Could you provide an analysis of this overhead, for instance, as a percentage of total training time or FLOPs?""</strong></em></p>
<p>Considering our targets of reducing both peak memory and FLOPs, our calibration needs to be efficient:</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Calibration does not increase peak memory</strong> thanks to on-policy processing several batches at once, and smaller batch setup <em>(line 766 main paper)</em></li>
<li><strong>Small calibration computation</strong> thanks to long-term subspaces utilization, which is proved via the following analysis of the ratio between calibration FLOP <mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="175" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-mo class="mjx-n"><mjx-c class="mjx-c28"></mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D453 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D459 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D45C TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-msub><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D45D TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em;"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i" size="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D450 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-script></mjx-msub><mjx-mo class="mjx-n"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29"></mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>f</mi><mi>l</mi><mi>o</mi><msub><mi>p</mi><mi>c</mi></msub><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container> and training FLOP <mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="176" style="font-size: 121.3%;
